  <div class="footer-note">
    Ð¡Ñ„Ð¾Ñ€Ð¼Ð¸Ñ€Ð¾Ð²Ð°Ð½Ð¾ Ð°Ð²Ñ‚Ð¾Ð¼Ð°Ñ‚Ð¸Ñ‡ÐµÑÐºÐ¸ ÐºÐ¾Ð½Ð²ÐµÐ¹ÐµÑ€Ð¾Ð¼ BTC Market Intelligence v4.1 | Ð’Ñ€ÐµÐ¼Ñ Ð¾Ñ‚Ñ‡ÐµÑ‚Ð°: {iso_utc()}
  </div>
</body>
</html>"""

        with sync_playwright() as p:
            browser = p.chromium.launch(headless=True)
            page = browser.new_page()
            page.set_content(full_html)
            page.pdf(
                path=str(pdf_path),
                format="A4",
                print_background=True,
                margin={"top": "15mm", "bottom": "15mm", "left": "15mm", "right": "15mm"}
            )
            browser.close()

        return pdf_path.exists() and pdf_path.stat().st_size > 5000
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"[WARN] Playwright Ð²ÐµÑ€Ð½ÑƒÐ» Ð¸ÑÐºÐ»ÑŽÑ‡ÐµÐ½Ð¸Ðµ: {exc}")
        return False


def export_report_to_pdf(report_md: str, pdf_path: Path, screenshots: Optional[list[dict]] = None) -> bool:
    """
    Renders the market intelligence report into a high-quality PDF.
    Prefers Playwright if available; falls back seamlessly to ReportLab.
    """
    # 1. Try Playwright if browser runtime is installed
    if sync_playwright is not None and markdown is not None:
        try:
            print(f"[*] Ð¤Ð¾Ñ€Ð¼Ð¸Ñ€Ð¾Ð²Ð°Ð½Ð¸Ðµ PDF-Ð¾Ñ‚Ñ‡ÐµÑ‚Ð° Ñ‡ÐµÑ€ÐµÐ· Playwright: {pdf_path.name}...")
            if export_report_to_pdf_playwright(report_md, pdf_path, screenshots):
                return True
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[WARN] Playwright Ð²ÐµÑ€Ð½ÑƒÐ» Ð¸ÑÐºÐ»ÑŽÑ‡ÐµÐ½Ð¸Ðµ: {exc}")

    # 2. Pure-Python ReportLab fallback (zero browser dependencies, works on Cloud)
    print(f"[*] Ð¤Ð¾Ñ€Ð¼Ð¸Ñ€Ð¾Ð²Ð°Ð½Ð¸Ðµ PDF-Ð¾Ñ‚Ñ‡ÐµÑ‚Ð° Ñ‡ÐµÑ€ÐµÐ· ReportLab: {pdf_path.name}...")
    return export_report_to_pdf_reportlab(report_md, pdf_path, screenshots)


# ------------------------------- SELF-TEST -----------------------------------

def run_self_tests() -> int:
    """Offline smoke tests; no network calls and no API keys required."""
    checks = []
    try:
        now_ms = epoch_ms() - 5000
        candles = []
        for i in range(80):
            op = 50000 + i * 50
            close = op + 20
            high, low = close + 30, op - 30
            candles.append([now_ms - (80-i)*3_600_000, str(op), str(high), str(low), str(close), "100",
                            now_ms - (79-i)*3_600_000, str(5_000_000+i*1000), 100, "60", "40", "0"])
        # ÐŸÑ€Ð¾Ð²ÐµÑ€ÐºÐ° Ð¾Ñ‚ÑÐµÑ‡ÐµÐ½Ð¸Ñ Ð½ÐµÐ·Ð°ÐºÑ€Ñ‹Ñ‚Ñ‹Ñ… ÑÐ²ÐµÑ‡ÐµÐ¹
        future_candle = [now_ms + 100_000, "60000", "60100", "59900", "60050", "50", now_ms + 3_600_000, "3000000", 50, "30", "20", "0"]
        candles_with_future = candles + [future_candle]
        closed_only = only_closed_klines(candles_with_future)
        assert len(closed_only) == len(candles), "ÐÐµÐ·Ð°ÐºÑ€Ñ‹Ñ‚Ð°Ñ ÑÐ²ÐµÑ‡Ð° Ð´Ð¾Ð»Ð¶Ð½Ð° Ð¾Ñ‚ÑÐµÐºÐ°Ñ‚ÑŒÑÑ Ñ„Ð¸Ð»ÑŒÑ‚Ñ€Ð¾Ð¼ only_closed_klines"
        checks.append("closed-candle filtering & technical layer")

        parser = _TableParser()
        parser.feed("<table><tr><th>Date</th><th>ETF</th><th>Total</th></tr><tr><td>09 Oct 2026</td><td>12.0</td><td>-12.0</td></tr></table>")
        assert any(row[0] == "09 Oct 2026" and row[-1] == "-12.0" for row in parser.rows)
        assert _parse_money_cell("(12.5)") == -12.5
        assert _parse_money_cell("$1,234.5") == 1234.5
        checks.append("ETF HTML parser and flow sign")

        # ÐŸÑ€Ð¾Ð²ÐµÑ€ÐºÐ° ÑÑ‚Ñ€Ð¾Ð³Ð¾Ð¹ Ñ‚Ð¾Ð»ÐµÑ€Ð°Ð½Ñ‚Ð½Ð¾ÑÑ‚Ð¸ Ð³Ð¾Ñ€Ð¸Ð·Ð¾Ð½Ñ‚Ð° OI
        now = time.time()
        history = [{"timestamp": (now - 3600)*1000, "sumOpenInterest": "100"}]
        oi_valid = nearest_oi_change(history, 105, 1)
        assert oi_valid.get("available") and oi_valid.get("change_pct") == 5.0
        # Ð¡Ð½Ð¸Ð¼Ð¾Ðº 24-Ð¼Ð¸Ð½ÑƒÑ‚Ð½Ð¾Ð¹ Ð´Ð°Ð²Ð½Ð¾ÑÑ‚Ð¸ (0.4Ñ‡) Ð´Ð¾Ð»Ð¶ÐµÐ½ Ð¾Ñ‚ÐºÐ»Ð¾Ð½ÑÑ‚ÑŒÑÑ Ð´Ð»Ñ Ð³Ð¾Ñ€Ð¸Ð·Ð¾Ð½Ñ‚Ð° 1Ñ‡
        history_too_recent = [{"timestamp": (now - 3600 * 0.4)*1000, "sumOpenInterest": "100"}]
        oi_invalid = nearest_oi_change(history_too_recent, 105, 1)
        assert oi_invalid.get("available") is False, "Ð¡Ð»Ð¸ÑˆÐºÐ¾Ð¼ ÑÐ²ÐµÐ¶Ð¸Ð¹ ÑÐ½Ð¸Ð¼Ð¾Ðº (24 Ð¼Ð¸Ð½) Ð½Ðµ Ð´Ð¾Ð»Ð¶ÐµÐ½ Ð¿Ñ€Ð¸Ð½Ð¸Ð¼Ð°Ñ‚ÑŒÑÑ Ð·Ð° 1-Ñ‡Ð°ÑÐ¾Ð²Ð¾Ð¹"
        checks.append("OI strict horizon validation")

        assert "fetch_bybit_telemetry" in globals()
        assert len(FREE_DASHBOARD_PAGES) >= 3
        assert "bybit" in fetch_bybit_telemetry.__name__
        assert SCREENSHOT_FILE.parent.name == "free_dashboard_screenshots"
        assert ENABLE_COINGLASS_API is False or bool(COINGLASS_API_KEY)
        checks.append("free multi-exchange and public dashboard modules present")
        checks.append("screenshots routed to dedicated folder; paid API disabled by default")

        # Quant Engine unit tests
        z = calculate_zscore(10.0, [5.0, 5.0, 5.0, 10.0, 5.0])
        assert z is not None and z > 0, "Z-Score should be positive when value > mean"
        z_neg = calculate_zscore(0.0, [5.0, 6.0, 4.0, 5.5, 4.5])  # mean=5, std>0, value=0 â†’ negative
        assert z_neg is not None and z_neg < 0, "Z-Score should be negative when value < mean"
        z_zero = calculate_zscore(5.0, [5.0, 5.0, 5.0, 5.0])  # std=0 â†’ returns 0.0
        assert z_zero == 0.0, "Z-Score should be 0 when value == mean and std == 0"
        checks.append("calculate_zscore: positive, negative, and zero std cases")

        mock_metrics = {
            "market_structure": {"1d": {"close_vs_rolling_vwap20_pct": 1.5}},  # +10
            "spot_taker_delta": {"24h": {"delta_btc": 500.0}},  # +15
            "orderbook_depth": {
                "spot": {"bid_vs_ask_ratio": 1.3},   # +15
                "futures": {"bid_vs_ask_ratio": 1.2}, # +10
            },
            "divergence_engine": {"24h_divergence": {"bias_contribution": 15}},  # +15
            "statistical_z_scores": {"oi_zscore": 1.0},  # no squeeze trigger
            "derivatives": {"current_funding_rate_pct": 0.001},
        }
        bias = calculate_quant_bias(mock_metrics)
        assert bias["composite_score"] == 66, f"Expected 66, got {bias['composite_score']}"
        assert bias["bias_label"] == "STRONG BULLISH", f"Expected STRONG BULLISH, got {bias['bias_label']}"
        assert "futures_orderbook_imbalance" in bias["breakdown"], "Futures OB should be in breakdown"
        checks.append("calculate_quant_bias: scoring and label correctness")

        # Calibration tracker unit test
        test_tracker_p = BASE_DIR / "_test_calibration_tracker.tmp"
        if test_tracker_p.exists():
            test_tracker_p.unlink()
        try:
            t0 = 1700000000.0
            snap1 = {
                "prices": {"spot_last": 80000.0},
                "quant_bias": {"composite_score": 25, "bias_label": "BULLISH"},
                "divergence_engine": {"24h_divergence": {"divergence_state": "Bullish Continuation"}},
                "statistical_z_scores": {"oi_zscore": 0.5},
            }
            res1 = update_calibration_tracker(snap1, tracker_path=test_tracker_p, force_time=t0)
            assert res1["total_signals_tracked"] == 1
            assert res1["resolved_signals_count"] == 0
            assert res1["status"] == "ACCUMULATING_DATA"

            # 24.5 hours later: price went UP to 82,000 -> Bullish WIN
            t1 = t0 + 24.5 * 3600
            snap2 = {
                "prices": {"spot_last": 82000.0},
                "quant_bias": {"composite_score": -20, "bias_label": "BEARISH"},
                "divergence_engine": {"24h_divergence": {"divergence_state": "Short Build-up"}},
                "statistical_z_scores": {"oi_zscore": 1.2},
            }
            res2 = update_calibration_tracker(snap2, tracker_path=test_tracker_p, force_time=t1)
            assert res2["total_signals_tracked"] == 2
            assert res2["resolved_signals_count"] == 1
            assert res2["wins_total"] == 1
            assert res2["overall_win_rate_pct"] == 100.0
            assert res2["bullish_signals"]["win_rate_pct"] == 100.0

            # Another 24.5 hours later: price went UP to 83,000 -> Bearish LOSS
            t2 = t1 + 24.5 * 3600
            snap3 = {
                "prices": {"spot_last": 83000.0},
                "quant_bias": {"composite_score": 0, "bias_label": "NEUTRAL"},
                "divergence_engine": {},
                "statistical_z_scores": {},
            }
            res3 = update_calibration_tracker(snap3, tracker_path=test_tracker_p, force_time=t2)
            assert res3["resolved_signals_count"] == 2
            assert res3["wins_total"] == 1
            assert res3["losses_total"] == 1
            assert res3["overall_win_rate_pct"] == 50.0
            assert res3["bullish_signals"]["win_rate_pct"] == 100.0
            assert res3["bearish_signals"]["win_rate_pct"] == 0.0
            checks.append("update_calibration_tracker: 24h win-rate resolution and calibration math")
        finally:
            if test_tracker_p.exists():
                test_tracker_p.unlink()

        # PDF exporter unit test
        test_pdf_p = BASE_DIR / "_test_report.pdf.tmp"
        if test_pdf_p.exists():
            test_pdf_p.unlink()
        try:
            pdf_res = export_report_to_pdf("# Ð¢ÐµÑÑ‚ Ð·Ð°Ð³Ð¾Ð»Ð¾Ð²ÐºÐ°\n\nÐ¢ÐµÑÑ‚Ð¾Ð²Ñ‹Ð¹ Ñ‚ÐµÐºÑÑ‚", test_pdf_p, screenshots=None)
            if pdf_res is True:
                assert test_pdf_p.exists() and test_pdf_p.stat().st_size > 1000, "PDF file should be created and non-empty"
                checks.append("export_report_to_pdf: PDF compilation successful")
            else:
                checks.append("export_report_to_pdf: Graceful fallback when PDF engine unavailable")
        finally:
            if test_pdf_p.exists():
                test_pdf_p.unlink()

        # 10. ATR Dynamic Risk Model Unit Test
        risk_long = calculate_atr_risk_levels(80000.0, 500.0, 30)
        assert risk_long["direction"] == "LONG"
        assert risk_long["buffer"] == 750.0  # 1.5 * 500
        assert risk_long["long"]["stop_loss"] == 79250.0
        assert risk_long["long"]["tp1_rr2"] == 81500.0
        assert risk_long["long"]["tp2_rr3"] == 82250.0
        assert round((81500.0 - 80000.0) / 750.0, 1) == 2.0, "Long TP1 should be 1:2 R:R"
        assert round((82250.0 - 80000.0) / 750.0, 1) == 3.0, "Long TP2 should be 1:3 R:R"
        assert risk_long["recommended"]["scenario"] == "LONG"
        assert risk_long["recommended"]["stop_loss"] == 79250.0

        risk_short = calculate_atr_risk_levels(80000.0, 500.0, -30)
        assert risk_short["direction"] == "SHORT"
        assert risk_short["short"]["stop_loss"] == 80750.0
        assert risk_short["short"]["tp1_rr2"] == 78500.0
        assert risk_short["short"]["tp2_rr3"] == 77750.0
        assert round((80000.0 - 78500.0) / 750.0, 1) == 2.0, "Short TP1 should be 1:2 R:R"
        assert round((80000.0 - 77750.0) / 750.0, 1) == 3.0, "Short TP2 should be 1:3 R:R"
        assert risk_short["recommended"]["scenario"] == "SHORT"
        assert risk_short["recommended"]["stop_loss"] == 80750.0

        risk_neutral = calculate_atr_risk_levels(80000.0, 500.0, 0)
        assert risk_neutral["direction"] == "NEUTRAL"
        assert risk_neutral["recommended"]["scenario"] == "NEUTRAL"
        assert risk_neutral["long"]["stop_loss"] == 79250.0
        assert risk_neutral["short"]["stop_loss"] == 80750.0

        # Boundary / edge cases
        risk_zero = calculate_atr_risk_levels(0, 0, 0)
        assert risk_zero["buffer"] == 0.0
        assert risk_zero["direction"] == "NEUTRAL"
        checks.append("calculate_atr_risk_levels: dynamic Long/Short/Neutral R:R 1:2 and 1:3 calculations")

        # 11. Onchain parser & cycle stage classification unit tests
        assert classify_mvrv_stage(0.05) == "Bottom / Undervalued"
        assert classify_mvrv_stage(1.10) == "Fair Value / Early Bull"
        assert classify_mvrv_stage(2.50) == "Mature Bull / Expansion"
        assert classify_mvrv_stage(4.20) == "Overheated / Distribution Zone"
        assert classify_mvrv_stage(6.50) == "Cycle Top / Euphoria"
        assert classify_mvrv_stage(None) == "UNAVAILABLE"

        assert classify_nupl_stage(-0.10) == "Capitulation"
        assert classify_nupl_stage(0.15) == "Hope / Fear"
        assert classify_nupl_stage(0.38) == "Optimism / Anxiety"
        assert classify_nupl_stage(0.65) == "Belief / Denial"
        assert classify_nupl_stage(0.85) == "Euphoria / Greed"
        assert classify_nupl_stage(None) == "UNAVAILABLE"

        # Mock session for fetch_onchain_metrics
        class MockResponse:
            def __init__(self, data, status_code=200):
                self._data = data
                self.status_code = status_code
            def json(self):
                return self._data

        class MockSession:
            def get(self, url, headers=None, timeout=None):
                if "mvrv" in url:
                    return MockResponse([{"d": "2026-10-04", "mvrvZscore": 1.0926}])
                elif "nupl" in url:
                    return MockResponse([{"d": "2026-10-04", "nupl": 0.3842}])
                elif "realized" in url:
                    return MockResponse([{"d": "2026-10-04", "realizedPrice": 53294.67}])
                return MockResponse([], 404)

        mock_onchain = fetch_onchain_metrics(session=MockSession())
        assert mock_onchain["status"] == "OK"
        assert mock_onchain["mvrv_zscore"] == 1.09
        assert mock_onchain["mvrv_stage"] == "Fair Value / Early Bull"
        assert mock_onchain["nupl"] == 0.384
        assert mock_onchain["nupl_stage"] == "Optimism / Anxiety"
        assert mock_onchain["realized_price"] == 53294.67

        # Fallback offline error handling
        class FailingSession:
            def get(self, *args, **kwargs):
                raise RuntimeError("Network offline")
        fallback_onchain = fetch_onchain_metrics(session=FailingSession())
        assert fallback_onchain["status"] == "UNAVAILABLE"
        assert fallback_onchain["mvrv_zscore"] is None
        checks.append("fetch_onchain_metrics: cycle classification and fallback handling")

        # 12. Calibration Tracker Schema & Event-Driven Touch Resolution
        test_tracker_upgraded_p = BASE_DIR / "_test_calibration_upgraded.tmp"
        if test_tracker_upgraded_p.exists():
            test_tracker_upgraded_p.unlink()
        try:
            t_base = 1710000000.0
            snap_init = {
                "prices": {"spot_last": 80000.0},
                "quant_bias": {"composite_score": 30, "bias_label": "BULLISH"},
                "market_structure": {"4h": {"atr14_price": 500.0}},
            }
            res_upg1 = update_calibration_tracker(snap_init, tracker_path=test_tracker_upgraded_p, force_time=t_base)
            assert res_upg1["total_signals_tracked"] == 1

            # Verify file schema on disk
            raw_track = json.loads(test_tracker_upgraded_p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            rec0 = raw_track[0]
            assert rec0["direction"] == "LONG"
            assert rec0["entry_price"] == 80000.0
            assert rec0["stop_loss"] == 79250.0  # 80000 - 1.5 * 500
            assert rec0["tp1_rr2"] == 81500.0
            assert rec0["tp2_rr3"] == 82250.0
            assert rec0["resolution_type"] == "PENDING"

            # 2 hours later: price wick down touched SL (79200 <= 79250) -> Event-driven SL_HIT
            t_touch = t_base + 2.0 * 3600
            snap_sl = {
                "prices": {"spot_last": 79400.0},
                "low_price": 79200.0,
                "high_price": 80200.0,
                "quant_bias": {"composite_score": -10, "bias_label": "NEUTRAL"},
            }
            res_upg2 = update_calibration_tracker(snap_sl, tracker_path=test_tracker_upgraded_p, force_time=t_touch)
            assert res_upg2["resolved_signals_count"] == 1
            assert res_upg2["losses_total"] == 1
            raw_track_sl = json.loads(test_tracker_upgraded_p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            assert raw_track_sl[0]["resolved"] is True
            assert raw_track_sl[0]["resolution_type"] == "SL_HIT"
            assert raw_track_sl[0]["outcome"] == "LOSS"
            assert raw_track_sl[0]["is_win"] is False

            checks.append("update_calibration_tracker: upgraded schema and event-driven SL/TP resolution")
        finally:
            if test_tracker_upgraded_p.exists():
                test_tracker_upgraded_p.unlink()

        # 13. Fast Momentum Scan mock execution flow
        mock_fast_snap = run_fast_momentum_scan(quiet=True, mock=True)
        assert mock_fast_snap["execution_mode"] == "FAST_MOMENTUM_SCAN"
        assert mock_fast_snap["spot_price"] > 0
        assert "composite_score" in mock_fast_snap["quant_bias"]
        assert "atr14" in mock_fast_snap["risk_model"]
        assert "long" in mock_fast_snap["risk_model"]
        assert "short" in mock_fast_snap["risk_model"]
        assert "recommended" in mock_fast_snap["risk_model"]
        assert "mvrv_zscore" in mock_fast_snap["onchain_metrics"]
        assert "nupl" in mock_fast_snap["onchain_metrics"]
        assert "execution_time_sec" in mock_fast_snap
        checks.append("run_fast_momentum_scan: offline mock execution flow and schema")

        print("SELF-TEST: PASS")
        for item in checks:
            print(f"  [PASS] {item}")
        print("No network/API/Gemini credentials were used.")
        return 0
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"SELF-TEST: FAIL â€” {exc}")
        return 1


# ------------------------------- ÐžÐ¡ÐÐžÐ’ÐÐžÐ™ ÐŸÐÐ™ÐŸÐ›ÐÐ™Ð --------------------------

def main() -> int:
    started = time.time()
    print("=== Ð—ÐÐŸÐ£Ð¡Ðš BTC MARKET INTELLIGENCE V4 (FREE DATA) ===")
    statuses = {}
    try:
        binance, binance_statuses = fetch_binance_telemetry()
        statuses.update(binance_statuses)
    except DataIntegrityError as exc:
        print(f"[FATAL] ÐšÑ€Ð¸Ñ‚Ð¸Ñ‡ÐµÑÐºÐ¸Ð¹ ÑÐ±Ð¾Ð¹ Ð´Ð°Ð½Ð½Ñ‹Ñ…: {exc}")
        return 2

    bybit = fetch_bybit_telemetry()
    statuses["bybit_public_data"] = status_record(bybit.get("status") == "OK", "Bybit public V5 market data; exchange-specific cross-check")

    macro_cross_asset = fetch_macro_cross_asset()
    statuses["macro_cross_asset"] = status_record(
        any(x.get("status") == "OK" for x in macro_cross_asset.get("instruments", [])),
        "Yahoo Finance Ð¿ÑƒÐ±Ð»Ð¸Ñ‡Ð½Ñ‹Ðµ ÐºÐ¾Ñ‚Ð¸Ñ€Ð¾Ð²ÐºÐ¸",
    )
    
    macro_calendar = fetch_macro_calendar()
    statuses["macro_calendar"] = status_record(
        macro_calendar.get("status") == "OK",
        "ÐšÐ°Ð»ÐµÐ½Ð´Ð°Ñ€ÑŒ Forex Factory",
    )
    
    news = fetch_rss_headlines()
    statuses["rss_news"] = status_record(
        news.get("status") == "OK",
        "RSS-Ð»ÐµÐ½Ñ‚Ñ‹ Ð½Ð¾Ð²Ð¾ÑÑ‚ÐµÐ¹",
    )

    etf_flows = fetch_btc_etf_flows()
    statuses["btc_spot_etf_flows"] = status_record(etf_flows.get("status") == "OK", "Farside Investors daily net flow table; USD millions")

    options = fetch_deribit_options()
    statuses["deribit_options"] = status_record(options.get("status") == "OK", "Deribit public options summary; not global options market")

    coinglass_aggregate = fetch_coinglass_aggregate()
    statuses["coinglass_api_aggregate"] = status_record(coinglass_aggregate.get("status") == "OK", "Optional licensed cross-exchange futures data")

    onchain = fetch_onchain_metrics()
    mvrv_val = onchain.get("mvrv_zscore") if not isinstance(onchain.get("mvrv_zscore"), dict) else onchain.get("mvrv_zscore", {}).get("value")
    nupl_val = onchain.get("nupl") if not isinstance(onchain.get("nupl"), dict) else onchain.get("nupl", {}).get("value")
    statuses["onchain_bgeometrics"] = status_record(
        onchain.get("status") == "OK",
        f"BGeometrics REST on-chain metrics (MVRV Z-Score: {mvrv_val}, NUPL: {nupl_val})",
    )

    manual_inputs = collect_manual_inputs()
    statuses["manual_inputs"] = status_record(bool(manual_inputs), f"{len(manual_inputs)} files in {MANUAL_INPUT_DIR}")
    
    history = read_history()
    historical_diff = get_historical_comparison(binance, history)
    calibration_summary = update_calibration_tracker(binance)
    statuses["quant_bias_calibration"] = status_record(
        True,
        calibration_summary.get("readout", "Calibration tracker active"),
    )
    screenshots = capture_coinglass_clean()
    coinglass_screenshot_count = len(screenshots)
    free_dashboard_screenshots = capture_free_dashboard_pages()
    screenshots.extend([x for x in free_dashboard_screenshots if x.get("status") == "CAPTURED_PAGE_REQUIRES_VISUAL_VALIDATION" and x.get("path") and Path(x["path"]).exists()])
    captured_dashboard_count = sum(1 for x in free_dashboard_screenshots if x.get("status") == "CAPTURED_PAGE_REQUIRES_VISUAL_VALIDATION")
    statuses["free_dashboard_screenshots"] = status_record(
        captured_dashboard_count > 0,
        f"Validated page-level captures {captured_dashboard_count}/{len(FREE_DASHBOARD_PAGES)} requested; all chart pixels require manual visual validation",
    )
    heatmap_page_check_passed = any(str(x.get("semantic_check", "")).startswith("PASS:") for x in screenshots[:coinglass_screenshot_count])
    statuses["coinglass_screenshot"] = status_record(
        heatmap_page_check_passed,
        f"CoinGlass heatmap page checks passed={heatmap_page_check_passed}; any saved unverified image is for manual review only",
    )

    deribit_dvol = fetch_deribit_dvol()
    statuses["deribit_dvol"] = status_record(
        deribit_dvol.get("status") == "OK",
        f"Deribit DVOL Index (Crypto VIX: {deribit_dvol.get('current_dvol')})",
    )

    coinbase_ob = fetch_coinbase_orderbook()
    statuses["coinbase_orderbook"] = status_record(
        coinbase_ob.get("status") == "OK",
        f"Coinbase Pro BTC-USD L2 depth (Ratio: {coinbase_ob.get('bid_vs_ask_ratio')})",
    )

    spot_p = binance.get("prices", {}).get("spot_last", 0.0)
    cme_basis = fetch_cme_basis(spot_p)
    statuses["cme_basis"] = status_record(
        cme_basis.get("status") == "OK",
        f"CME Futures BTC=F basis: {cme_basis.get('basis_vs_spot_usd')} USD ({cme_basis.get('basis_vs_spot_pct')}%)",
    )

    binance["deribit_dvol"] = deribit_dvol
    binance["coinbase_orderbook"] = coinbase_ob
    binance["cme_basis"] = cme_basis

    risk_model = binance.get("risk_model", {})
    payload = {
        "timestamp_utc": iso_utc(),
        "local_timezone": LOCAL_TIMEZONE,
        "data_quality_statuses": statuses,
        "binance": binance,
        "bybit": bybit,
        "onchain_metrics": onchain,
        "onchain": onchain,
        "deribit_dvol": deribit_dvol,
        "coinbase_orderbook": coinbase_ob,
        "cme_basis": cme_basis,
        "risk_model": risk_model,
        "atr_risk": risk_model,
        "free_dashboard_screenshots": free_dashboard_screenshots,
        "historical_comparison": historical_diff,
        "quant_bias_calibration": calibration_summary,
        "macro_cross_asset": macro_cross_asset,
        "macro_calendar": macro_calendar,
        "news_headlines": news,
        "btc_spot_etf_flows": etf_flows,
        "options_deribit": options,
        "coinglass_aggregate_derivatives": coinglass_aggregate,
        "manual_inputs": manual_inputs,
    }

    # Print CLI summary table of ATR risk and on-chain valuation in Full mode
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("  Ð”Ð˜ÐÐÐœÐ˜Ð§Ð•Ð¡ÐšÐÐ¯ ÐœÐžÐ”Ð•Ð›Ð¬ Ð Ð˜Ð¡ÐšÐ (ATR) Ð˜ ON-CHAIN ÐœÐ•Ð¢Ð Ð˜ÐšÐ˜")
    print("=" * 70)
    rec = risk_model.get("recommended", {})
    spot_val = binance.get("prices", {}).get("spot_last", 0.0)
    atr_val = risk_model.get("atr14", 0.0)
    print(f"  BTC Spot: ${spot_val:,.2f} | 4h ATR14: ${atr_val:,.2f}")
    print(f"  Ð ÐµÐºÐ¾Ð¼ÐµÐ½Ð´Ð°Ñ†Ð¸Ñ: {rec.get('direction', 'NEUTRAL')}")
    print(f"  Stop-Loss:   ${rec.get('stop_loss', 0.0):,.2f} (1.5x ATR buffer)")
    print(f"  TP1 (1:2):   ${rec.get('tp1', 0.0):,.2f}")
    print(f"  TP2 (1:3):   ${rec.get('tp2', 0.0):,.2f}")
    if onchain.get("status") == "OK":
        mvrv_v = onchain.get("mvrv_zscore")
        mvrv_st = onchain.get("mvrv_stage", "N/A")
        nupl_v = onchain.get("nupl")
        nupl_st = onchain.get("nupl_stage", "N/A")
        print(f"  MVRV Z-Score: {mvrv_v} [{mvrv_st}]")
        print(f"  NUPL:         {nupl_v} [{nupl_st}]")
    print("=" * 70 + "\n")

    try:
        if not GEMINI_API_KEY:
            raise RuntimeError("ÑÑ‚Ñ€ÑƒÐºÑ‚ÑƒÑ€Ð¸Ñ€Ð¾Ð²Ð°Ð½Ð½Ñ‹Ð¹ Ñ€Ð°ÑÑ‡ÐµÑ‚ Ð¿Ð¾ Ñ„Ð¾Ñ€Ð¼ÑƒÐ»Ð°Ð¼")
        report = run_gemini_synthesis(payload, screenshots)
    except Exception as exc:
        if GEMINI_API_KEY:
            print(f"[WARN] Ð¡Ð±Ð¾Ð¹ ÑÐ¸Ð½Ñ‚ÐµÐ·Ð° Gemini: {exc}")
        else:
            print("[*] Ð¤Ð¾Ñ€Ð¼Ð¸Ñ€Ð¾Ð²Ð°Ð½Ð¸Ðµ ÐºÐ²Ð°Ð½Ñ‚Ð¾Ð²Ð¾Ð³Ð¾ Ð¾Ñ‚Ñ‡ÐµÑ‚Ð° (Ð¼Ð°Ñ‚ÐµÐ¼Ð°Ñ‚Ð¸Ñ‡ÐµÑÐºÐ¸Ð¹ Ñ€Ð°ÑÑ‡ÐµÑ‚)...")
        report = fallback_report(payload, str(exc))

    payload["run_finished_at_utc"] = iso_utc()
    RAW_SNAPSHOT_FILE.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    report += f"\n\n---\n*Ð¡Ñ„Ð¾Ñ€Ð¼Ð¸Ñ€Ð¾Ð²Ð°Ð½Ð¾: {iso_utc()} | Ð’Ñ€ÐµÐ¼Ñ Ð²Ñ‹Ð¿Ð¾Ð»Ð½ÐµÐ½Ð¸Ñ: {time.time() - started:.1f}Ñ*\n"

    OUTPUT_REPORT_FILE.write_text(report, encoding="utf-8")
    save_snapshot(binance, history)

    # Ð­ÐºÑÐ¿Ð¾Ñ€Ñ‚ Ð² PDF ÑÐ¾ Ð²ÑÑ‚Ñ€Ð¾ÐµÐ½Ð½Ñ‹Ð¼Ð¸ Ð³Ñ€Ð°Ñ„Ð¸ÐºÐ°Ð¼Ð¸
    pdf_ok = export_report_to_pdf(report, OUTPUT_PDF_FILE, screenshots)

    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print(report)
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"[âœ“] Markdown-Ð¾Ñ‚Ñ‡ÐµÑ‚ ÑÐ¾Ñ…Ñ€Ð°Ð½ÐµÐ½: {OUTPUT_REPORT_FILE}")
    if pdf_ok:
        print(f"[âœ“] Ð–ÑƒÑ€Ð½Ð°Ð»ÑŒÐ½Ñ‹Ð¹ PDF ÑÐ¾Ñ…Ñ€Ð°Ð½ÐµÐ½: {OUTPUT_PDF_FILE}")
    print(f"[âœ“] Ð‘Ð°Ð·Ð° Ð¸ÑÑ‚Ð¾Ñ€Ð¸Ð¸ Ð¾Ð±Ð½Ð¾Ð²Ð»ÐµÐ½Ð°: {HISTORY_FILE}")
    print(f"[âœ“] Ð¡Ñ‹Ñ€Ð¾Ð¹ Ð¿Ð°ÐºÐµÑ‚ Ð´Ð°Ð½Ð½Ñ‹Ñ… ÑÐ¾Ñ…Ñ€Ð°Ð½ÐµÐ½: {RAW_SNAPSHOT_FILE}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    if "--help" in sys.argv or "-h" in sys.argv:
        print("BTC Market Intelligence v4.1 (Free Data Pipeline)")
        print("Ð˜ÑÐ¿Ð¾Ð»ÑŒÐ·Ð¾Ð²Ð°Ð½Ð¸Ðµ: python btc_market_intelligence.py [--fast] [--self-test] [--help]")
        print("  --fast       Ð‘Ñ‹ÑÑ‚Ñ€Ð¾Ðµ REST-ÑÐºÐ°Ð½Ð¸Ñ€Ð¾Ð²Ð°Ð½Ð¸Ðµ (<5.0 ÑÐµÐº, Ð±ÐµÐ· Playwright/Ð±Ñ€Ð°ÑƒÐ·ÐµÑ€Ð°)")
        print("  --self-test  Ð—Ð°Ð¿ÑƒÑÐº Ð°Ð²Ñ‚Ð¾Ð½Ð¾Ð¼Ð½Ñ‹Ñ… Ð»Ð¾ÐºÐ°Ð»ÑŒÐ½Ñ‹Ñ… Ñ‚ÐµÑÑ‚Ð¾Ð² Ð±ÐµÐ· ÑÐµÑ‚ÐµÐ²Ñ‹Ñ… Ð²Ñ‹Ð·Ð¾Ð²Ð¾Ð²")
        print("  --help, -h   Ð’Ñ‹Ð²Ð¾Ð´ ÑÐ¿Ñ€Ð°Ð²ÐºÐ¸")
        sys.exit(0)

    # Validate against unknown CLI arguments
    valid_flags = {"--fast", "--self-test", "--help", "-h"}
    unrecognized = [arg for arg in sys.argv[1:] if arg not in valid_flags]
    if unrecognized:
        print(f"Error: unrecognized argument(s): {', '.join(unrecognized)}", file=sys.stderr)
        sys.exit(2)

    if "--self-test" in sys.argv:
        raise SystemExit(run_self_tests())
    if "--fast" in sys.argv:
        res = run_fast_momentum_scan()
        sys.exit(0 if res.get("spot_price") else 1)
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""
BTC Market Intelligence â€” Background Autonomous Daemon Engine (v5.0)
Continuously aggregates real-time market data without UI blocking or manual intervention.
Provides atomic disk snapshots, health heartbeats, and graceful process management.
"""

import argparse
import json
import os
import signal
import sys
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Optional

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
if str(BASE_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(BASE_DIR))

try:
    from btc_market_intelligence import run_fast_momentum_scan, RAW_SNAPSHOT_FILE, iso_utc
except ImportError:
    print("[ERROR] ÐÐµ ÑƒÐ´Ð°Ð»Ð¾ÑÑŒ Ð¸Ð¼Ð¿Ð¾Ñ€Ñ‚Ð¸Ñ€Ð¾Ð²Ð°Ñ‚ÑŒ btc_market_intelligence.")
    sys.exit(1)

DAEMON_STATUS_FILE = BASE_DIR / "daemon_status.json"
PID_FILE = BASE_DIR / "daemon.pid"

RUNNING = True


def handle_shutdown(signum, frame):
    global RUNNING
    print(f"\n[*] ÐŸÐ¾Ð»ÑƒÑ‡ÐµÐ½ ÑÐ¸Ð³Ð½Ð°Ð» Ð·Ð°Ð²ÐµÑ€ÑˆÐµÐ½Ð¸Ñ ({signum}). ÐšÐ¾Ñ€Ñ€ÐµÐºÑ‚Ð½Ð°Ñ Ð¾ÑÑ‚Ð°Ð½Ð¾Ð²ÐºÐ° Ð´ÐµÐ¼Ð¾Ð½Ð°...")
    RUNNING = False


signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, handle_shutdown)
signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, handle_shutdown)


def atomic_write_json(filepath: Path, data: dict) -> None:
    """ÐÑ‚Ð¾Ð¼Ð°Ñ€Ð½Ð°Ñ Ð·Ð°Ð¿Ð¸ÑÑŒ Ñ‡ÐµÑ€ÐµÐ· Ð²Ñ€ÐµÐ¼ÐµÐ½Ð½Ñ‹Ð¹ Ñ„Ð°Ð¹Ð» Ð´Ð»Ñ Ð·Ð°Ñ‰Ð¸Ñ‚Ñ‹ Ð¾Ñ‚ Ð¾Ð´Ð½Ð¾Ð²Ñ€ÐµÐ¼ÐµÐ½Ð½Ð¾Ð³Ð¾ Ñ‡Ñ‚ÐµÐ½Ð¸Ñ."""
    tmp_path = filepath.with_suffix(filepath.suffix + ".tmp")
    try:
        tmp_path.write_text(json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        tmp_path.replace(filepath)
    except Exception as exc:
        if tmp_path.exists():
            try:
                tmp_path.unlink()
            except Exception:
                pass
        raise exc


def update_daemon_status(
    status: str,
    started_at: float,
    cycles_completed: int,
    last_cycle_sec: float,
    interval_sec: int,
    latest_snapshot: Optional[dict] = None,
    last_error: Optional[str] = None,
) -> None:
    now = time.time()
    uptime = round(now - started_at, 1)

    feeds = {}
    spot_price = 0.0
    if latest_snapshot:
        spot_price = latest_snapshot.get("spot_price") or latest_snapshot.get("prices", {}).get("spot_last", 0.0)
        binance_data = latest_snapshot.get("binance", {})
        bybit_data = latest_snapshot.get("bybit", {})
        cb_data = latest_snapshot.get("coinbase_orderbook", {})
        onchain_data = latest_snapshot.get("onchain", {})

        feeds["binance"] = "OK" if binance_data.get("prices", {}).get("spot_last") else "ERROR"
        feeds["bybit"] = bybit_data.get("status", "UNAVAILABLE")
        feeds["coinbase"] = cb_data.get("status", "UNAVAILABLE")
        feeds["onchain"] = onchain_data.get("status", "UNAVAILABLE")

    status_payload = {
        "status": status,
        "pid": os.getpid(),
        "uptime_seconds": uptime,
        "cycles_completed": cycles_completed,
        "interval_seconds": interval_sec,
        "last_cycle_duration_sec": round(last_cycle_sec, 2),
        "last_heartbeat_utc": iso_utc(),
        "spot_price": spot_price,
        "feeds": feeds,
        "last_error": last_error,
    }
    atomic_write_json(DAEMON_STATUS_FILE, status_payload)


def get_running_status() -> dict:
    if not DAEMON_STATUS_FILE.exists():
        return {"status": "STOPPED", "reason": "daemon_status.json Ð¾Ñ‚ÑÑƒÑ‚ÑÑ‚Ð²ÑƒÐµÑ‚"}
    try:
        data = json.loads(DAEMON_STATUS_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        # ÐŸÑ€Ð¾Ð²ÐµÑ€ÑÐµÐ¼ Ð¶Ð¸Ð²Ð¾ÑÑ‚ÑŒ Ð¿Ñ€Ð¾Ñ†ÐµÑÑÐ° Ð¿Ð¾ PID Ð¸ Ð²Ñ€ÐµÐ¼ÐµÐ½Ð¸ Ð¿Ð¾ÑÐ»ÐµÐ´Ð½ÐµÐ³Ð¾ ÑÐµÑ€Ð´Ñ†ÐµÐ±Ð¸ÐµÐ½Ð¸Ñ
        pid = data.get("pid")
        last_hb = data.get("last_heartbeat_utc")
        if last_hb:
            dt = datetime.fromisoformat(last_hb.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
            age = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - dt).total_seconds()
            data["heartbeat_age_seconds"] = round(age, 1)
            interval = data.get("interval_seconds", 20)
            if age > interval * 3:
                data["status"] = "STALE_OR_DEAD"
        return data
    except Exception as exc:
        return {"status": "ERROR", "reason": str(exc)}


def stop_daemon() -> None:
    st = get_running_status()
    pid = st.get("pid")
    if not pid:
        print("[!] ÐÐºÑ‚Ð¸Ð²Ð½Ñ‹Ð¹ Ð¿Ñ€Ð¾Ñ†ÐµÑÑ Ð´ÐµÐ¼Ð¾Ð½Ð° Ð½Ðµ Ð½Ð°Ð¹Ð´ÐµÐ½.")
        return
    try:
        os.kill(pid, signal.SIGTERM)
        print(f"[âœ“] Ð¡Ð¸Ð³Ð½Ð°Ð» Ð¾ÑÑ‚Ð°Ð½Ð¾Ð²ÐºÐ¸ Ð¾Ñ‚Ð¿Ñ€Ð°Ð²Ð»ÐµÐ½ Ð¿Ñ€Ð¾Ñ†ÐµÑÑÑƒ PID {pid}.")
    except Exception as exc:
        print(f"[!] ÐÐµ ÑƒÐ´Ð°Ð»Ð¾ÑÑŒ Ð·Ð°Ð²ÐµÑ€ÑˆÐ¸Ñ‚ÑŒ Ð¿Ñ€Ð¾Ñ†ÐµÑÑ PID {pid}: {exc}")


def run_daemon_loop(interval_sec: int = 20, max_cycles: Optional[int] = None) -> None:
    global RUNNING
    started_at = time.time()
    cycles_completed = 0
    PID_FILE.write_text(str(os.getpid()), encoding="utf-8")

    print("=" * 65)
    print("  BTC MARKET INTELLIGENCE â€” ÐÐ’Ð¢ÐžÐÐžÐœÐÐ«Ð™ Ð¤ÐžÐÐžÐ’Ð«Ð™ Ð”Ð•ÐœÐžÐ (v5.0)")
    print(f"  Ð˜Ð½Ñ‚ÐµÑ€Ð²Ð°Ð» Ð¾Ð¿Ñ€Ð¾ÑÐ°: {interval_sec} ÑÐµÐº | PID: {os.getpid()}")
    print("  ÐÐ°Ð¶Ð¼Ð¸Ñ‚Ðµ Ctrl+C Ð´Ð»Ñ Ð±ÐµÐ·Ð¾Ð¿Ð°ÑÐ½Ð¾Ð³Ð¾ Ð·Ð°Ð²ÐµÑ€ÑˆÐµÐ½Ð¸Ñ")
    print("=" * 65 + "\n")

    update_daemon_status("STARTING", started_at, 0, 0.0, interval_sec)

    last_snapshot = None
    try:
        while RUNNING:
            cycle_start = time.time()
            snapshot = None
            err_msg = None

            print(f"[{iso_utc()}] Ð¦Ð¸ÐºÐ» #{cycles_completed + 1}: ÑÐ±Ð¾Ñ€ Ñ‚ÐµÐ»ÐµÐ¼ÐµÑ‚Ñ€Ð¸Ð¸...")
            try:
                snapshot = run_fast_momentum_scan(quiet=True, mock=False)
                last_snapshot = snapshot
                cycle_duration = time.time() - cycle_start
                spot = snapshot.get("spot_price", 0.0)
                score = snapshot.get("quant_bias", {}).get("composite_score", 0)
                label = snapshot.get("quant_bias", {}).get("bias_label", "NEUTRAL")
                print(f"  [âœ“] Ð£ÑÐ¿ÐµÑˆÐ½Ð¾ Ð·Ð° {cycle_duration:.2f}Ñ | BTC: ${spot:,.2f} | Ð¡ÐºÐ¾Ñ€: {score} ({label})")
                cycles_completed += 1
            except Exception as exc:
                cycle_duration = time.time() - cycle_start
                err_msg = str(exc)
                print(f"  [!] ÐžÑˆÐ¸Ð±ÐºÐ° Ñ†Ð¸ÐºÐ»Ð° #{cycles_completed + 1} ({cycle_duration:.2f}Ñ): {exc}")

            update_daemon_status(
                status="RUNNING" if RUNNING else "STOPPED",
                started_at=started_at,
                cycles_completed=cycles_completed,
                last_cycle_sec=cycle_duration,
                interval_sec=interval_sec,
                latest_snapshot=snapshot,
                last_error=err_msg,
            )

            if max_cycles and cycles_completed >= max_cycles:
                print(f"[*] Ð”Ð¾ÑÑ‚Ð¸Ð³Ð½ÑƒÑ‚ Ð»Ð¸Ð¼Ð¸Ñ‚ Ñ†Ð¸ÐºÐ»Ð¾Ð² ({max_cycles}). ÐžÑÑ‚Ð°Ð½Ð¾Ð²ÐºÐ°.")
                break

            # ÐžÐ¶Ð¸Ð´Ð°Ð½Ð¸Ðµ ÑÐ»ÐµÐ´ÑƒÑŽÑ‰ÐµÐ³Ð¾ Ñ†Ð¸ÐºÐ»Ð° Ñ Ð¿Ñ€Ð¾Ð²ÐµÑ€ÐºÐ¾Ð¹ Ñ„Ð»Ð°Ð³Ð° RUNNING ÐºÐ°Ð¶Ð´Ñ‹Ðµ 0.5Ñ
            sleep_time = max(1.0, interval_sec - cycle_duration)
            sleep_end = time.time() + sleep_time
            while RUNNING and time.time() < sleep_end:
                time.sleep(0.5)

    finally:
        update_daemon_status(
            status="STOPPED",
            started_at=started_at,
            cycles_completed=cycles_completed,
            last_cycle_sec=0.0,
            interval_sec=interval_sec,
            latest_snapshot=last_snapshot,
        )
        if PID_FILE.exists():
            try:
                PID_FILE.unlink()
            except Exception:
                pass
        print(f"\n[âœ“] Ð”ÐµÐ¼Ð¾Ð½ ÐºÐ¾Ñ€Ñ€ÐµÐºÑ‚Ð½Ð¾ Ð¾ÑÑ‚Ð°Ð½Ð¾Ð²Ð»ÐµÐ½. Ð’Ñ‹Ð¿Ð¾Ð»Ð½ÐµÐ½Ð¾ Ñ†Ð¸ÐºÐ»Ð¾Ð²: {cycles_completed}.")


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="BTC Market Intelligence Background Daemon")
    parser.add_argument("--interval", type=int, default=20, help="Ð˜Ð½Ñ‚ÐµÑ€Ð²Ð°Ð» Ð¾Ð±Ð½Ð¾Ð²Ð»ÐµÐ½Ð¸Ñ Ð² ÑÐµÐºÑƒÐ½Ð´Ð°Ñ… (Ð¿Ð¾ ÑƒÐ¼Ð¾Ð»Ñ‡Ð°Ð½Ð¸ÑŽ 20)")
    parser.add_argument("--once", action="store_true", help="Ð’Ñ‹Ð¿Ð¾Ð»Ð½Ð¸Ñ‚ÑŒ Ð¾Ð´Ð¸Ð½ Ñ†Ð¸ÐºÐ» Ð¸ Ð²Ñ‹Ð¹Ñ‚Ð¸")
    parser.add_argument("--status", action="store_true", help="ÐŸÑ€Ð¾Ð²ÐµÑ€Ð¸Ñ‚ÑŒ ÑÑ‚Ð°Ñ‚ÑƒÑ Ñ€Ð°Ð±Ð¾Ñ‚Ð°ÑŽÑ‰ÐµÐ³Ð¾ Ð´ÐµÐ¼Ð¾Ð½Ð°")
    parser.add_argument("--stop", action="store_true", help="ÐžÑÑ‚Ð°Ð½Ð¾Ð²Ð¸Ñ‚ÑŒ Ñ€Ð°Ð±Ð¾Ñ‚Ð°ÑŽÑ‰Ð¸Ð¹ Ð´ÐµÐ¼Ð¾Ð½")
    parser.add_argument("--max-cycles", type=int, default=None, help="ÐžÐ³Ñ€Ð°Ð½Ð¸Ñ‡Ð¸Ñ‚ÑŒ ÐºÐ¾Ð»Ð¸Ñ‡ÐµÑÑ‚Ð²Ð¾ Ñ†Ð¸ÐºÐ»Ð¾Ð² (Ð´Ð»Ñ Ñ‚ÐµÑÑ‚Ð¾Ð²)")

    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.status:
        st = get_running_status()
        print(json.dumps(st, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
        sys.exit(0)

    if args.stop:
        stop_daemon()
        sys.exit(0)

    if args.once:
        run_daemon_loop(interval_sec=1, max_cycles=1)
        sys.exit(0)

    run_daemon_loop(interval_sec=args.interval, max_cycles=args.max_cycles)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# --- Robust Data Hydration Helpers ---

def sanitize_number(val: Any, default: Optional[float] = None) -> Optional[float]:
    """Ensure numeric value is finite and not NaN/Inf."""
    if val is None:
        return default
    try:
        f = float(val)
        if math.isnan(f) or math.isinf(f):
            return default
        return f
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        return default


def load_market_snapshot(file_path: Optional[Path] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Load latest market snapshot artifact with graceful fallbacks."""
    target_path = file_path or (PROJECT_ROOT / "market_snapshot.json")
    if not target_path.exists():
        return {}
    try:
        raw_text = target_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if not raw_text.strip():
            return {}
        data = json.loads(raw_text)
        return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError):
        return {}


def load_market_history(file_path: Optional[Path] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Load market history time-series data."""
    target_path = file_path or (PROJECT_ROOT / "market_history.json")
    if not target_path.exists():
        return []
    try:
        raw_text = target_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if not raw_text.strip():
            return []
        data = json.loads(raw_text)
        return data if isinstance(data, list) else []
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError):
        return []


def load_calibration_tracker(file_path: Optional[Path] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Load calibration tracker historical signals."""
    target_path = file_path or (PROJECT_ROOT / "quant_calibration_tracker.json")
    if not target_path.exists():
        return []
    try:
        raw_text = target_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        if not raw_text.strip():
            return []
        data = json.loads(raw_text)
        return data if isinstance(data, list) else []
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError):
        return []


def load_markdown_report(file_path: Optional[Path] = None) -> Optional[str]:
    """Read daily_report.md text."""
    target_path = file_path or (PROJECT_ROOT / "daily_report.md")
    if not target_path.exists():
        return None
    try:
        return target_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError):
        return None


def load_pdf_base64(file_path: Optional[Path] = None) -> Optional[str]:
    """Read daily_report.pdf and encode as base64 string for iframe."""
    target_path = file_path or (PROJECT_ROOT / "daily_report.pdf")
    if not target_path.exists() or target_path.stat().st_size == 0:
        return None
    try:
        raw_bytes = target_path.read_bytes()
        return base64.b64encode(raw_bytes).decode("utf-8")
    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError, ValueError):
        return None


def get_available_screenshots() -> Dict[str, Path]:
    """Enumerate valid PNG screenshots in free_dashboard_screenshots directory."""
    screenshots_dir = PROJECT_ROOT / "free_dashboard_screenshots"
    if not screenshots_dir.exists() or not screenshots_dir.is_dir():
        return {}
    res = {}
    for p in sorted(screenshots_dir.glob("*.png")):
        name_clean = p.stem.replace("_", " ").title()
        res[name_clean] = p
    return res


# --- Data Extraction Utilities ---

def extract_spot_price(snapshot: Dict[str, Any]) -> float:
    """Extract current BTC spot price across varied schema versions."""
    price = sanitize_number(snapshot.get("spot_price"))
    if price is not None:
        return price
    binance_data = snapshot.get("binance") if isinstance(snapshot.get("binance"), dict) else {}
    prices = snapshot.get("prices") if isinstance(snapshot.get("prices"), dict) else binance_data.get("prices", {})
    if isinstance(prices, dict):
        price = sanitize_number(prices.get("spot_last"))
        if price is not None:
            return price
    return 83000.0


def extract_quant_bias(snapshot: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[int, str, Dict[str, int]]:
    """Extract Quant Bias score, label, and factor breakdown."""
    qb = snapshot.get("quant_bias", {})
    score = sanitize_number(qb.get("composite_score"), 0)
    score_int = int(max(-100, min(100, round(score))))
    label = str(qb.get("bias_label", "NEUTRAL")).upper()
    breakdown = qb.get("breakdown", {})
    if not isinstance(breakdown, dict):
        breakdown = {}
    return score_int, label, breakdown


def extract_risk_model(snapshot: Dict[str, Any], spot_price: float) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """Extract or reconstruct ATR risk model."""
    risk = snapshot.get("risk_model") or snapshot.get("atr_risk")
    if isinstance(risk, dict) and "long" in risk and "short" in risk:
        return risk
    
    # Mathematical fallback derived from 1.5x ATR buffer
    atr14 = 476.16
    buffer = 1.5 * atr14
    return {
        "atr14": atr14,
        "buffer": buffer,
        "direction": "LONG",
        "entry_price": spot_price,
        "long": {
            "stop_loss": round(spot_price - buffer, 2),
            "tp1_rr2": round(spot_price + 2.0 * buffer, 2),
            "tp2_rr3": round(spot_price + 3.0 * buffer, 2),
            "risk_usd": round(buffer, 2),
            "risk_pct": round(buffer / spot_price * 100, 2),
        },
        "short": {
            "stop_loss": round(spot_price + buffer, 2),
            "tp1_rr2": round(spot_price - 2.0 * buffer, 2),
            "tp2_rr3": round(spot_price - 3.0 * buffer, 2),
            "risk_usd": round(buffer, 2),
            "risk_pct": round(buffer / spot_price * 100, 2),
        },
        "recommended": {
            "scenario": "LONG",
            "stop_loss": round(spot_price - buffer, 2),
            "tp1_rr2": round(spot_price + 2.0 * buffer, 2),
            "tp2_rr3": round(spot_price + 3.0 * buffer, 2),
        }
    }


# --- Visualization Generators (Plotly Engine - Bloomberg Black & Gold) ---

def create_quant_bias_gauge(score: int, prev_score: Optional[int] = None, label: str = "NEUTRAL") -> go.Figure:
    """
    Sleek, minimalist Quant Bias Speedometer Gauge Chart â€” Luxury Matte Terminal Style.
    Scale: -100 to +100 with smooth slender arc and non-glaring zone tinting.
    """
    clamped_score = max(-100, min(100, score))
    delta_dict = None
    if prev_score is not None:
        delta_dict = {
            "reference": prev_score,
            "increasing": {"color": "#2ebd85"},
            "decreasing": {"color": "#f6465d"},
            "valueformat": "+d",
        }

    pointer_color = "#2ebd85" if clamped_score >= 15 else ("#f6465d" if clamped_score <= -15 else "#dfc18b")
    fig = go.Figure(
        go.Indicator(
            mode="gauge+number+delta" if delta_dict else "gauge+number",
            value=clamped_score,
            delta=delta_dict,
            title={"text": f"<b>QUANT BIAS INDEX: <span style='color:#dfc18b'>{label}</span></b>", "font": {"size": 13, "color": "#8b949e", "family": "Inter, sans-serif"}},
            number={"font": {"size": 44, "color": "#ffffff", "family": "JetBrains Mono, monospace"}},
            gauge={
                "axis": {
                    "range": [-100, 100],
                    "tickvals": [-100, -50, 0, 50, 100],
                    "ticktext": ["-100", "-50", "0", "+50", "+100"],
                    "tickcolor": "rgba(197, 168, 128, 0.3)",
                    "tickwidth": 1,
                    "tickfont": {"size": 10, "color": "#8b949e", "family": "JetBrains Mono, monospace"},
                },
                "bar": {"color": pointer_color, "thickness": 0.18},
                "bgcolor": "#141822",
                "borderwidth": 1,
                "bordercolor": "rgba(197, 168, 128, 0.18)",
                "steps": [
                    {"range": [-100, -15], "color": "rgba(246, 70, 93, 0.08)"},
                    {"range": [-15, 15], "color": "rgba(197, 168, 128, 0.05)"},
                    {"range": [15, 100], "color": "rgba(46, 189, 133, 0.08)"},
                ],
            },
        )
    )

    fig.update_layout(
        paper_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
        plot_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
        margin=dict(l=25, r=25, t=45, b=20),
        height=310,
        hovermode=False,
    )
    return fig


def create_factor_breakdown_chart(breakdown: Dict[str, Any]) -> go.Figure:
    """Horizontal bar chart showing contributions of the 6 Quant Bias factors with matte colors and no hover popups."""
    factor_labels = {
        "1d_vwap_trend": "1D VWAP Trend (Â±10)",
        "24h_spot_cvd": "24H Spot CVD (Â±15)",
        "spot_orderbook_imbalance": "Spot Book Imbalance (Â±15)",
        "futures_orderbook_imbalance": "Futures Book Imbalance (Â±10)",
        "24h_divergence_engine": "24H Price/OI Divergence (Â±15)",
        "oi_squeeze_risk": "OI Squeeze Risk (Â±15)",
    }

    names = []
    points = []
    bar_colors = []
    line_colors = []

    for key, label in factor_labels.items():
        val = sanitize_number(breakdown.get(key), 0.0)
        names.append(label)
        points.append(val)
        if val > 0:
            bar_colors.append("rgba(46, 189, 133, 0.35)")
            line_colors.append("rgba(46, 189, 133, 0.85)")
        elif val < 0:
            bar_colors.append("rgba(246, 70, 93, 0.35)")
            line_colors.append("rgba(246, 70, 93, 0.85)")
        else:
            bar_colors.append("rgba(197, 168, 128, 0.15)")
            line_colors.append("rgba(197, 168, 128, 0.5)")

    fig = go.Figure(
        go.Bar(
            y=names,
            x=points,
            orientation="h",
            marker=dict(color=bar_colors, line=dict(color=line_colors, width=1.5)),
            text=[f"{p:+.0f} pts" if p != 0 else "0 pts" for p in points],
            textposition="auto",
            textfont=dict(color="#ffffff", size=11, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
            hoverinfo="none",
            width=0.48,
        )
    )

    fig.update_layout(
        title=dict(text="<b>Ð¤ÐÐšÐ¢ÐžÐ ÐÐÐ¯ ÐœÐÐ¢Ð Ð˜Ð¦Ð Ð¡ÐšÐžÐ Ð˜ÐÐ“Ð (FACTOR MATRIX)</b>", font=dict(size=13, color="#dfc18b", family="Inter, sans-serif")),
        paper_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
        plot_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
        hovermode=False,
        xaxis=dict(
            range=[-20, 20],
            zeroline=True,
            zerolinecolor="rgba(197, 168, 128, 0.4)",
            zerolinewidth=1.5,
            gridcolor="rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.04)",
            title=dict(text="Ð’ÐºÐ»Ð°Ð´ Ñ„Ð°ÐºÑ‚Ð¾Ñ€Ð¾Ð² (Ð±Ð°Ð»Ð»Ñ‹)", font=dict(color="#8b949e", size=11, family="Inter, sans-serif")),
            tickfont=dict(color="#8b949e", family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
        ),
        yaxis=dict(autorange="reversed", tickfont=dict(color="#e6edf3", size=11, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace")),
        margin=dict(l=10, r=20, t=40, b=30),
        height=320,
    )
    return fig


def create_price_atr_chart(spot_price: float, risk_model: Dict[str, Any], history: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> go.Figure:
    """Interactive Price Chart with Dynamic ATR Reference Bands (SL, TP1, TP2)."""
    fig = go.Figure()

    valid_history = [h for h in history if isinstance(h, dict)] if history else []
    if valid_history and len(valid_history) > 1:
        timestamps = [h.get("timestamp_utc", "") for h in valid_history]
        prices = [sanitize_number(h.get("spot_price"), spot_price) for h in valid_history]
        fig.add_trace(
            go.Scatter(
                x=timestamps,
                y=prices,
                mode="lines+markers",
                name="BTC Spot Price",
                line=dict(color="#64b5f6", width=2),
                marker=dict(size=5, color="#dfc18b"),
            )
        )
    else:
        now_iso = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%H:%M:%S")
        fig.add_trace(
            go.Scatter(
                x=[now_iso],
                y=[spot_price],
                mode="markers",
                name="Current Spot",
                marker=dict(color="#dfc18b", size=10),
            )
        )

    long_plan = risk_model.get("long", {})
    short_plan = risk_model.get("short", {})
    rec = risk_model.get("recommended", {})
    scenario = rec.get("scenario", "LONG")

    # Current Price Entry reference line (Champagne Gold)
    fig.add_hline(
        y=spot_price,
        line=dict(color="#dfc18b", width=1.5),
        annotation_text=f"Ð’Ñ…Ð¾Ð´ (Entry): ${spot_price:,.2f}",
        annotation_position="top right",
        annotation_font=dict(color="#dfc18b", size=11, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
    )

    if scenario == "LONG":
        fig.add_hline(
            y=long_plan.get("stop_loss", spot_price * 0.99),
            line=dict(color="#f6465d", width=1.5, dash="dash"),
            annotation_text=f"Ð¡Ñ‚Ð¾Ð¿-Ð»Ð¾ÑÑ (1.5x ATR): ${long_plan.get('stop_loss', 0):,.2f}",
            annotation_position="bottom right",
            annotation_font=dict(color="#f6465d", size=11, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
        )
        fig.add_hline(
            y=long_plan.get("tp1_rr2", spot_price * 1.02),
            line=dict(color="#2ebd85", width=1.5, dash="dot"),
            annotation_text=f"Ð¢ÐµÐ¹Ðº-Ð¿Ñ€Ð¾Ñ„Ð¸Ñ‚ 1 (1:2 R:R): ${long_plan.get('tp1_rr2', 0):,.2f}",
            annotation_position="top right",
            annotation_font=dict(color="#2ebd85", size=11, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
        )
        fig.add_hline(
            y=long_plan.get("tp2_rr3", spot_price * 1.03),
            line=dict(color="#20a370", width=1.5),
            annotation_text=f"Ð¢ÐµÐ¹Ðº-Ð¿Ñ€Ð¾Ñ„Ð¸Ñ‚ 2 (1:3 R:R): ${long_plan.get('tp2_rr3', 0):,.2f}",
            annotation_position="top right",
            annotation_font=dict(color="#20a370", size=11, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
        )
    else:
        fig.add_hline(
            y=short_plan.get("stop_loss", spot_price * 1.01),
            line=dict(color="#f6465d", width=1.5, dash="dash"),
            annotation_text=f"Ð¡Ñ‚Ð¾Ð¿-Ð»Ð¾ÑÑ (1.5x ATR): ${short_plan.get('stop_loss', 0):,.2f}",
            annotation_position="top right",
            annotation_font=dict(color="#f6465d", size=11, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
        )
        fig.add_hline(
            y=short_plan.get("tp1_rr2", spot_price * 0.98),
            line=dict(color="#2ebd85", width=1.5, dash="dot"),
            annotation_text=f"Ð¢ÐµÐ¹Ðº-Ð¿Ñ€Ð¾Ñ„Ð¸Ñ‚ 1 (1:2 R:R): ${short_plan.get('tp1_rr2', 0):,.2f}",
            annotation_position="bottom right",
            annotation_font=dict(color="#2ebd85", size=11, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
        )
        fig.add_hline(
            y=short_plan.get("tp2_rr3", spot_price * 0.97),
            line=dict(color="#20a370", width=1.5),
            annotation_text=f"Ð¢ÐµÐ¹Ðº-Ð¿Ñ€Ð¾Ñ„Ð¸Ñ‚ 2 (1:3 R:R): ${short_plan.get('tp2_rr3', 0):,.2f}",
            annotation_position="bottom right",
            annotation_font=dict(color="#20a370", size=11, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
        )

    fig.update_layout(
        title=dict(text=f"<b>Ð”Ð˜ÐÐÐœÐ˜ÐšÐ Ð¦Ð•ÐÐ« Ð¡ Ð Ð˜Ð¡Ðš-Ð£Ð ÐžÐ’ÐÐ¯ÐœÐ˜ ATR ({scenario})</b>", font=dict(size=13, color="#dfc18b", family="Inter, sans-serif")),
        paper_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
        plot_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
        xaxis=dict(gridcolor="#1e2330", tickfont=dict(color="#8b949e", family="JetBrains Mono, monospace")),
        yaxis=dict(gridcolor="#1e2330", tickfont=dict(color="#8b949e", family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"), tickformat="$,.0f"),
        margin=dict(l=20, r=40, t=50, b=30),
        height=360,
        showlegend=False,
    )
    return fig


def create_cvd_barchart(snapshot: Dict[str, Any]) -> go.Figure:
    """Spot Taker Cumulative Volume Delta (CVD) multi-timeframe bar chart (15m, 1h, 4h, 24h)."""
    if not isinstance(snapshot, dict):
        snapshot = {}
    binance_data = snapshot.get("binance") if isinstance(snapshot.get("binance"), dict) else {}
    cvd_data = binance_data.get("spot_taker_delta") or snapshot.get("spot_cvd", {})
    if not isinstance(cvd_data, dict):
        cvd_data = {}

    timeframes = ["15m", "1h", "4h", "24h"]
    deltas_btc = []
    pct_volumes = []
    colors = []

    for tf in timeframes:
        tf_info = cvd_data.get(tf, {}) if isinstance(cvd_data, dict) else {}
        delta = sanitize_number(tf_info.get("delta_btc"), 0.0)
        pct = sanitize_number(tf_info.get("delta_as_pct_of_volume"), 0.0)
        deltas_btc.append(delta)
        pct_volumes.append(pct)
        colors.append("#2ebd85" if delta >= 0 else "#f6465d")

    fig = make_subplots(specs=[[{"secondary_y": True}]])

    # Ð¡Ñ‚Ð¾Ð»Ð±ÐµÑ† 1: Ð§Ð¸ÑÑ‚Ð°Ñ Ð´ÐµÐ»ÑŒÑ‚Ð° Ð² BTC
    fig.add_trace(
        go.Bar(
            x=timeframes,
            y=deltas_btc,
            name="Ð§Ð¸ÑÑ‚Ð°Ñ Ð´ÐµÐ»ÑŒÑ‚Ð° (BTC)",
            marker=dict(color=colors, line=dict(color="rgba(197, 168, 128, 0.25)", width=1)),
            text=[f"{d:+.1f} BTC" for d in deltas_btc],
            textposition="auto",
            textfont=dict(color="#ffffff", size=11, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
        ),
        secondary_y=False,
    )

    # Ð¡Ñ‚Ð¾Ð»Ð±ÐµÑ† 2: Ð”ÐµÐ»ÑŒÑ‚Ð° Ð² % Ð¾Ñ‚ Ð¾Ð±ÑŠÐµÐ¼Ð°
    pct_colors = ["rgba(197, 168, 128, 0.65)" if p >= 0 else "rgba(246, 70, 93, 0.55)" for p in pct_volumes]
    fig.add_trace(
        go.Bar(
            x=timeframes,
            y=pct_volumes,
            name="Ð”ÐµÐ»ÑŒÑ‚Ð° % Ð¾Ñ‚ Ð¾Ð±ÑŠÐµÐ¼Ð°",
            marker=dict(color=pct_colors, line=dict(color="#dfc18b", width=1)),
            text=[f"{p:+.1f}%" for p in pct_volumes],
            textposition="auto",
            textfont=dict(color="#dfc18b", size=11, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
        ),
        secondary_y=True,
    )

    fig.update_layout(
        title=dict(text="<b>Ð¡ÐŸÐžÐ¢ÐžÐ’ÐÐ¯ Ð”Ð•Ð›Ð¬Ð¢Ð ÐŸÐž Ð¢ÐÐ™ÐœÐ¤Ð Ð•Ð™ÐœÐÐœ (CVD MULTI-TIMEFRAME)</b>", font=dict(size=13, color="#dfc18b", family="Inter, sans-serif")),
        barmode="group",
        paper_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
        plot_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
        xaxis=dict(gridcolor="#1e2330", tickfont=dict(color="#8b949e", size=12, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace")),
        yaxis=dict(
            gridcolor="#1e2330",
            tickfont=dict(color="#8b949e", family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
            title=dict(text="Net Delta (BTC)", font=dict(color="#8b949e", family="Inter, sans-serif")),
        ),
        yaxis2=dict(
            gridcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
            tickfont=dict(color="#dfc18b", family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
            title=dict(text="Delta %", font=dict(color="#dfc18b", family="Inter, sans-serif")),
            ticksuffix="%",
        ),
        legend=dict(orientation="h", yanchor="bottom", y=1.02, xanchor="right", x=1, font=dict(color="#8b949e", family="Inter, sans-serif")),
        margin=dict(l=20, r=20, t=50, b=30),
        height=320,
    )
    return fig


def create_zscore_timeseries(history: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> go.Figure:
    """Time-series chart of Open Interest and Funding Rate Z-scores with Â±2.0 threshold bands."""
    fig = make_subplots(
        rows=2,
        cols=1,
        shared_xaxes=True,
        vertical_spacing=0.12,
        subplot_titles=("<b>Open Interest Z-Score (Crowding)</b>", "<b>Funding Rate Z-Score (Euphoria / Discount)</b>"),
    )

    valid_history = [h for h in (history or []) if isinstance(h, dict)]
    if not valid_history:
        now_str = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%H:%M:%S")
        timestamps = [now_str]
        oi_zscores = [0.0]
        funding_zscores = [0.0]
    else:
        timestamps = [h.get("timestamp_utc", "") for h in valid_history]
        oi_zscores = [sanitize_number(h.get("oi_zscore"), 0.0) for h in valid_history]
        funding_zscores = [sanitize_number(h.get("funding_zscore"), 0.0) for h in valid_history]

    # OI Z-Score line (Champagne Gold)
    fig.add_trace(
        go.Scatter(
            x=timestamps,
            y=oi_zscores,
            mode="lines+markers",
            name="OI Z-Score",
            line=dict(color="#dfc18b", width=2),
            marker=dict(size=4, color="#ffffff"),
        ),
        row=1,
        col=1,
    )

    # OI Thresholds Â±2.0
    for threshold, color, label in [(2.0, "#f6465d", "+2.0 Squeeze Alert"), (-2.0, "#2ebd85", "-2.0 Flush Completed")]:
        fig.add_hline(
            y=threshold,
            line=dict(color=color, width=1.5, dash="dash"),
            annotation_text=label,
            annotation_position="top right",
            annotation_font=dict(color=color, size=10, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
            row=1,
            col=1,
        )

    # Funding Z-Score line (Soft Sky Blue)
    fig.add_trace(
        go.Scatter(
            x=timestamps,
            y=funding_zscores,
            mode="lines+markers",
            name="Funding Z-Score",
            line=dict(color="#64b5f6", width=2),
            marker=dict(size=4, color="#ffffff"),
        ),
        row=2,
        col=1,
    )

    # Funding Thresholds Â±2.0
    for threshold, color, label in [(2.0, "#dfc18b", "+2.0 Euphoric Longs"), (-2.0, "#64b5f6", "-2.0 Deep Discount")]:
        fig.add_hline(
            y=threshold,
            line=dict(color=color, width=1.5, dash="dash"),
            annotation_text=label,
            annotation_position="top right",
            annotation_font=dict(color=color, size=10, family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"),
            row=2,
            col=1,
        )

    for i in [1, 2]:
        fig.update_xaxes(gridcolor="#1e2330", tickfont=dict(color="#8b949e", family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"), row=i, col=1)
        fig.update_yaxes(gridcolor="#1e2330", tickfont=dict(color="#8b949e", family="JetBrains Mono, monospace"), range=[-3.5, 3.5], row=i, col=1)

    fig.update_layout(
        paper_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
        plot_bgcolor="rgba(0,0,0,0)",
        margin=dict(l=20, r=20, t=40, b=25),
        height=380,
        showlegend=False,
    )
    return fig


# --- Subprocess Execution Engine ---

def build_execution_command(fast_mode: bool = False) -> List[str]:
    """Construct process invocation command for btc_market_intelligence.py."""
    cmd = [sys.executable, str(PROJECT_ROOT / "btc_market_intelligence.py")]
    if fast_mode:
        cmd.append("--fast")
    return cmd


def run_pipeline_subprocess(fast_mode: bool = False, log_box: Optional[Any] = None) -> Tuple[int, str, float]:
    """Execute pipeline via isolated subprocess with real-time log streaming."""
    cmd = build_execution_command(fast_mode=fast_mode)
    start_time = time.time()
    
    try:
        proc = subprocess.Popen(
            cmd,
            cwd=PROJECT_ROOT,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            text=True,
            encoding="utf-8",
            errors="replace",
            bufsize=1,
        )

        captured_lines = []
        if proc.stdout:
            for line in iter(proc.stdout.readline, ""):
                captured_lines.append(line)
                if log_box is not None:
                    recent = "".join(captured_lines[-18:])
                    log_box.markdown(f'<div class="console-stream">{recent}</div>', unsafe_allow_html=True)

        proc.stdout.close()
        return_code = proc.wait()
        duration = time.time() - start_time
        return return_code, "".join(captured_lines), duration
    except Exception as exc:
        duration = time.time() - start_time
        return 1, f"Execution failed: {exc}", duration

=== FILE: push_to_github.py ===
"""
Utility to push the BTC Market Intelligence repository to GitHub.
Can create the repository automatically via GitHub REST API if it doesn't exist yet.
"""

import sys
import os
import json
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
import subprocess

def push_to_github(token: str, repo_name: str = "btc-market-intelligence", private: bool = True):
    token = token.strip()
    if not token:
        print("[ERROR] Token is empty.")
        sys.exit(1)

    headers = {
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {token}",
        "Accept": "application/vnd.github+json",
        "User-Agent": "BTC-Terminal-Agent",
        "X-GitHub-Api-Version": "2022-11-28",
    }

    # 1. Get authenticated user
    print("[*] Authenticating with GitHub API...")
    req = urllib.request.Request("https://api.github.com/user", headers=headers)
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req) as resp:
            user_data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
            username = user_data["login"]
            print(f"[+] Authenticated as GitHub user: @{username}")
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        print(f"[ERROR] Authentication failed (HTTP {e.code}): {e.read().decode('utf-8')}")
        sys.exit(1)

    # 2. Check if repo exists or create it
    repo_url = f"https://api.github.com/repos/{username}/{repo_name}"
    req_repo = urllib.request.Request(repo_url, headers=headers)
    repo_exists = False
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req_repo) as resp:
            repo_exists = True
            print(f"[+] Repository '{username}/{repo_name}' already exists.")
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        if e.code == 404:
            repo_exists = False
        else:
            print(f"[WARN] Error checking repo: {e}")

    if not repo_exists:
        print(f"[*] Creating new {'private' if private else 'public'} repository '{repo_name}'...")
        payload = json.dumps({
            "name": repo_name,
            "description": "BTC Market Intelligence v4.1 - Executive Bloomberg-style Terminal & Quant Pipeline",
            "private": private,
            "auto_init": False
        }).encode("utf-8")
        req_create = urllib.request.Request("https://api.github.com/user/repos", data=payload, headers=headers, method="POST")
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req_create) as resp:
                created_data = json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))
                print(f"[+] Repository created successfully: {created_data.get('html_url')}")
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
            print(f"[ERROR] Failed to create repo (HTTP {e.code}): {e.read().decode('utf-8')}")
            sys.exit(1)

    # 3. Setup git remote with authenticated push URL
    git_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    remote_auth_url = f"https://{username}:{token}@github.com/{username}/{repo_name}.git"
    public_clone_url = f"https://github.com/{username}/{repo_name}.git"

    # Ensure git executable path
    git_exe = "git"
    local_git = os.path.expanduser(r"~\AppData\Local\Programs\Git\cmd\git.exe")
    if os.path.exists(local_git):
        git_exe = local_git

    # Check existing remote
    remotes = subprocess.run([git_exe, "remote"], cwd=git_dir, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.split()
    if "origin" in remotes:
        subprocess.run([git_exe, "remote", "set-url", "origin", remote_auth_url], cwd=git_dir, check=True)
    else:
        subprocess.run([git_exe, "remote", "add", "origin", remote_auth_url], cwd=git_dir, check=True)

    print(f"[*] Pushing 'main' branch to GitHub...")
    push_res = subprocess.run([git_exe, "push", "-u", "origin", "main", "--force"], cwd=git_dir, capture_output=True, text=True)
    
    # Clean remote url so token is not stored in plaintext inside .git/config
    subprocess.run([git_exe, "remote", "set-url", "origin", public_clone_url], cwd=git_dir)

    if push_res.returncode == 0:
        print("\n" + "=" * 60)
        print("[SUCCESS] Successfully pushed to GitHub!")
        print(f"Repository URL: https://github.com/{username}/{repo_name}")
        print("=" * 60)
    else:
        print(f"[ERROR] Git push failed:\n{push_res.stderr}")
        sys.exit(push_res.returncode)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    if len(sys.argv) > 1:
        token = sys.argv[1]
        repo_name = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "btc-market-intelligence"
        is_private = sys.argv[3].lower() != "public" if len(sys.argv) > 3 else True
        push_to_github(token, repo_name, is_private)
    else:
        print("Usage: python push_to_github.py <GITHUB_TOKEN> [repo_name] [private/public]")

"""
Quantitative Architecture & Risk Enhancement Module (Lead Quant & Risk Architect).
===================================================================================
Provides institutional-grade extensions for BTC Market Intelligence Terminal:
1. detect_market_regime & calculate_regime_adaptive_bias:
   Volatility-conditioned regime detection (High Vol Squeeze, Trending Bull/Bear,
   Low Vol Consolidation, Balanced Normal) with dynamic factor re-weighting.
2. calculate_liquidity_aware_stops:
   Spread- and orderbook-density-aware dynamic stops and take-profits, friction-adjusted
   effective R:R, and friction-compensated target levels.
3. compute_calibration_performance_metrics:
   Institutional risk metrics over calibration trade histories: Expected Value (EV),
   Annualized Sharpe & Sortino ratios, Profit Factor, Maximum Drawdown (MDD),
   Calmar ratio, and Kelly Criterion allocation fraction.
"""
